00016: function formatCpf(v: string) {
00017:   v = v.replace(/\D/g, "");
00018:   if (v.length > 11) v = v.slice(0, 11);
00019:   return v.replace(/(\d{3})(\d)/, "$1.$2").replace(/(\d{3})(\d)/, "$1.$2").replace(/(\d{3})(\d{1,2})$/, "$1-$2");
00020: }
00021: 
00022: function formatPhone(v: string) {
00023:   v = v.replace(/\D/g, "");
00024:   if (v.length > 11) v = v.slice(0, 11);
00025:   if (v.length > 10) return v.replace(/(\d{2})(\d{5})(\d{4})/, "($1) $2-$3");
00026:   if (v.length > 6) return v.replace(/(\d{2})(\d{4})(\d{0,4})/, "($1) $2-$3");
00027:   if (v.length > 2) return v.replace(/(\d{2})(\d{0,5})/, "($1) $2");
00028:   return v;
00029: }
00030: 
00031: function calcAge(birthDate: string | undefined): number | null {
00032:   if (!birthDate) return null;
00033:   const birth = new Date(birthDate);
00034:   const today = new Date();
00035:   let age = today.getFullYear() - birth.getFullYear();
00036:   const m = today.getMonth() - birth.getMonth();
00037:   if (m < 0 || (m === 0 && today.getDate() < birth.getDate())) age--;
00038:   return age;
00039: }

00070: function calcAge(birthDate: string | undefined): number | null {
00071:   if (!birthDate) return null;

00073:   const today = new Date();

00076:   if (m < 0 || (m === 0 && today.getDate() < birth.getDate())) age--;
00077:   return age;

02900: export async function saveAppointment(app: Appointment) {
02901:   const { data: userData } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
02902:   if (!userData.user) throw new Error("Não autenticado");
02903: 
02904:   const dbObj = {
02905:     user_id: userData.user.id,
02906:     patient_id: app.patientId,
02907:     procedure_id: app.procedureId || null,
02908:     treatment_plan_id: app.treatmentPlanId || null,
02909:     title: app.title,
02910:     appointment_date: app.appointmentDate,
02911:     start_time: app.startTime,
02912:     end_time: app.endTime,
02913:     status: app.status || "agendado",
02914:     type: app.type || "consulta",
02915:     notes: app.notes || null,
02916:     whatsapp_reminder: app.whatsappReminder ?? false,
02917:   };
02918: 
02919:   if (app.id && !app.id.startsWith("temp-")) {
02920:     const { error } = await supabase
02921:       .from("appointments")
02922:       .update({ ...dbObj, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
02923:       .eq("id", app.id)
02924:       .eq("user_id", userData.user.id);
02925:     if (error) {
02926:       logSupabaseError("update appointments", error);
02927:       throw error;
02928:     }
02929:   } else {
02930:     const { error } = await supabase.from("appointments").insert(dbObj);

01873:     logSupabaseError("delete treatment_plan_items", error);
01874:     throw error;
01875:   }
01876: }

02617: export function usePayments(patientId?: string): [Payment[], boolean, () => void] {
02618:   const [payments, setPayments] = useState<Payment[]>([]);
02619:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
02620:   const [refreshTrigger, setRefreshTrigger] = useState(0);
02621:   const refetch = useCallback(() => setRefreshTrigger((value) => value + 1), []);
02622: 
02623:   useEffect(() => {
02624:     let active = true;
02625:     async function fetch() {
02626:       if (!patientId) {
02627:         if (active) {
02628:           setPayments([]);
02629:           setLoading(false);
02630:         }
02631:         return;
02632:       }
02633:       try {
02634:         const { data: userData } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
02635:         if (!userData.user) return;
02636: 
02637:         const { data, error } = await supabase
02638:           .from("payments")
02639:           .select("*, payment_splits(*)")
02640:           .eq("user_id", userData.user.id)
02641:           .eq("patient_id", patientId)
02642:           .order("payment_date", { ascending: false });
02643: 
02644:         if (error) {
02645:           if (error.code !== "42P01") throw error;
02646:           if (active) setLoading(false);
02647:           return;

--- PADRÃO: usePayments | LINHA: 2679 ---
02667:                 id: s.id,
02668:                 userId: s.user_id,

02671:                 amount: s.amount,
02672:                 createdAt: s.created_at,
02673:               })),
02674:             })),
02675:           );

02677:         }
02678:       } catch (err) {
02679:         logSupabaseError("usePayments", err);

02681:       }
02682:     }

02684:     return () => {
02685:       active = false;
02686:     };
02687:   }, [patientId, refreshTrigger]);
02688:
